- `c8e6`: Moves the bishop to e6. Blocks the check.
- `d8e7`: Moves the queen to e7. Blocks the check. This is usually a poor move as it puts the queen in a vulnerable position.
- `f8e7`: Moves the bishop to e7. Blocks the check.

Evaluation:
- `f6e4`: The knight on e4 is strongly placed, but let's check for captures. White has a queen on d4. The queen can capture e4. White also has a knight on f3. The knight can capture e4. So `f6e4` leads to a loss of material or a trade.
- `c8e6`: Bishop on e6. Is it attacked? Queen on d4 can capture e6.
- `f8e7`: Bishop on e7. Is it attacked? Queen on d4 cannot attack e7. Knight on f3 cannot attack e7.
- `d8e7`: Queen on e7. Is it attacked? Queen on d4 cannot attack e7. Knight on f3 cannot attack e7. But it's better to block with a bishop than the queen.
